--- AGENTS.md ---
     1	# AGENTS.md — Codex Implementer
     2	
     3	You are Codex, the **implementer** for Gold Rush, a three.js browser game. Claude (the Cowork session) is the orchestrator per `CLAUDE.md`: it slices specs, delegates one slice per task, reviews, integrates, commits, and owns `STATUS.md`. You implement exactly the task you are given — nothing more.
     4	
     5	(Orchestration was briefly Codex-owned 2026-07-03/04; Robin moved it back to Claude on 2026-07-04. Historical AGENTS.md guidance from that era is void.)
     6	
     7	## Read order
     8	
     9	1. The task you were given: goal, spec path, acceptance criteria, constraints, do-not-touch list.
    10	2. The active spec under `specs/`.
    11	3. `docs/GOLD_RUSH_BRIEF.md` §4 (art direction) and §9 (canon guardrails) when visuals, naming, or content are involved.
    12	4. `STATUS.md` §verification-lessons before writing or running tests.
    13	5. `assets/LEDGER.md` when generated art, slots, or prompts are involved.
    14	
    15	## Operating rules
    16	
    17	- One spec slice per task. If you find adjacent problems, report them; do not fix out of scope.
    18	- For queued MAIN-slot tasks only: if the runner claimed the task while `STATUS.md` line 1 was clear, a later FIRE `ACTIVE` lock does not revoke that claim; finish and report without polling for the lock to clear.
    19	- Do NOT touch `STATUS.md`, `specs/`, `reviews/`, existing e2e specs, or git history (no commits) unless the task explicitly says otherwise. The orchestrator integrates and records evidence.
    20	- Sandbox facts (binding when you run there): every bash call is interrupted/resumed; write files early; servers die between calls; NEVER `npx playwright install`; Playwright needs `LD_LIBRARY_PATH=~/locallibs/usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu`; reply `READY-FOR-GATES` when done — the supervisor runs the gates.
    21	
    22	## Quality bar (the orchestrator gates on these)
    23	
    24	- `npm run build` green; zero console/page errors; relevant Playwright specs pass; full regression whenever sim semantics change.
    25	- TypeScript + Vite + three.js only. No heavy frameworks. No secrets in client code.
    26	- Canon: frontier-tech weapons (rigs, beacons, brass/teal agent-tech — no realistic firearms); illustrated, never gory; no Native American enemies; public names are places/rituals, not backend tools.
    27	- **IMAGE GENERATION LAW (owner directive 2026-07-11): still images come from your NATIVE image_gen tool ONLY — it is free. NEVER generate images through the higgsfield CLI (no `gpt_image_2`, `nano_banana`, `flux_*`, or ANY higgsfield image model — those bill the owner's paid credits). Higgsfield is for VIDEO (seedance) only, and only when the task explicitly says so. If your native image tool is unavailable, STOP and report — do not substitute.**
    28	- Placeholder-first art; generated assets wire through `assets/layer-contracts/` + `assets/LEDGER.md`.
    29	- Ownership invariants: Economy is the only gold writer; CombatSystem the only damage resolver; one TargetingSystem; pools per family; every pickup floats its amount (e2e-asserted).
    30	
    31	## Model routing (owner ruling 2026-07-11, re-affirmed): the runner default and the AUTHORING default for CODEX headers = **gpt-5.6-sol @ medium**. gpt-5.5 headers are RESERVED for art batches and purely mechanical tasks only; sol@high for sim-critical; ultra = requested, never ambient.
    32	
    33	## Interactive co-agent sessions (GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra and successors) — added 2026-07-10, owner-directed
    34	
    35	If you are an INTERACTIVE session exploring this repo (not the lane runner executing a queued task), welcome — the factory laws above still bind, plus these:
    36	
    37	1. **Read first, always:** `CLAUDE.md` (the constitution), `docs/CONTENT-MAP.md` (the content/lore/media orientation — REQUIRED before any player-visible-text or art-registry work), `STATUS.md` line 1 (if it says ACTIVE with a stamp <45 min, a fire owns main's tree — do not write to it), `tasks/BACKLOG.md` (the complete work ledger).
    38	2. **Your output surface is the `sol/*` branch namespace.** Implement on `sol/<topic>` branches — never commit to main, never reset/force-push `lane/*` branches (the runner owns those). The orchestrator drains your branches exactly like lanes: gates on evidence, path-scoped merge, review file. A branch with tests and a READY-FOR-GATES note in its final commit message gets drained fastest.
    39	3. **Communicate through files, not chat memory:** findings → `reviews/sol-findings-<topic>.md` (F-IDs, file:line, evidence); task proposals → `tasks/PROPOSED-<name>.md` (never copy into `tasks/queue/*` yourself — queueing is the orchestrator's act); design explorations → `docs/proposals/`. Anything not in a file does not exist.
    40	4. **Never:** `git add -A` at repo root · edit `STATUS.md` · mark BACKLOG items shipped (report, don't bookkeep) · edit ratified rulings in `specs/` or `lore/` (propose supersessions instead) · run destructive git on shared branches · start dev servers on ports 5188/5199/8788/8799 (gate + rig ports).
    41	5. **Good first work for a strong interactive model:** the PERF-ARCHITECTURE audit (BACKLOG ladder: instancing coverage, draw-call batching, texture memory, geometry pooling — measure with the perf harnesses, then propose); design proposals for GT-06 build-pads or the E3 power-graph engine (bundle: `specs/epoch-saga/e3-voltage-bundle.md`); adversarial review of anything in `reviews/` you disagree with.

--- CLAUDE.md ---
     1	# Gold Rush — Operating Manual
     2	### The constitution for ANY orchestrating model in this repo. Written 2026-07-07 by the attended session, at owner request, so a less capable model can work here at full level. When you don't know what to do, this file does.
     3	
     4	## 0. What this is, in one breath
     5	A three.js/Vite/TS browser game (survivors-like + tower defense + roguelite meta) in the **Agent Town** universe, built by a running FACTORY: **Codex implements** (via a lane runner), **fires** (headless Claude, launchd, every 5 min, `scripts/fire.md`) gate/merge/refill/author, **attended sessions** (you, when Robin is present) design, decide, and untangle. Robin owns and verdicts. M0/M1/M2 signed off. The ten-epoch saga is specced (`specs/epoch-saga/`). Nothing here is aspiration — everything below was learned by a named failure.
     6	
     7	## 1. Where truth lives (read in this order at session start)
     8	1. `STATUS.md` **line 1 only** — the lock + the current state. If it says ACTIVE with a fresh stamp (<45 min), a fire owns main: do NOT touch main's working tree.
     9	2. `tasks/BACKLOG.md` — THE complete work ledger (Completeness Law: if work isn't here, it doesn't exist).
    10	3. `docs/HANDOVER-2026-07-20.md` §4 — in-flight notes + standing orders.
    11	4. `bash scripts/health-watch.sh status` — the live board in 2 seconds. (`logs/dashboard.html` is the same, prettier.) ⛔ **A HEADLESS FIRE CANNOT RUN THAT AS WRITTEN — `bash` is in neither settings allowlist (89 entries, zero for bash; measured s2350, F-2350-1), and a fire has nobody to approve the prompt, so it is REFUSED where an attended session is merely asked. Route it through node, which IS allowlisted:** `node -e "const{execFileSync}=require('child_process');console.log(execFileSync('bash',['scripts/health-watch.sh','status'],{encoding:'utf8'}))"` **— the gate denies YOU, not the factory. Same shape for every `bash scripts/*.sh` this repo prescribes; see `scripts/fire.md` §2.0d.**
    12	5. When touching design/art/canon: `docs/GOLD_RUSH_BRIEF.md` §4+§9, `docs/decisions/ADR-001..003`, the relevant spec in `specs/`.
    13	NEVER trust a claim you inherited (see Mistake #4). Verify with a command before acting on it.
    14	
    15	## 2. The cast and their boundaries
    16	- **Codex (runner)**: implements EXACTLY one task file; commits on its lane branch (runner auto-commits since the fd9efee patch); never touches STATUS/reviews/other lanes. Reads `AGENTS.md`.
    17	- **Fires**: obey `scripts/fire.md` (their own law file — do not duplicate it here). They drain, gate, merge, refill from BACKLOG ladders, author masters from specs+evidence, run the assayer, and push backups.
    18	- **Attended (you)**: everything the fires escalate — specs, design forks, canon, untangles, owner conversation. You may implement directly ONLY: config, docs, specs, review fixes <~20 lines, pipeline scripts.
    19	- **Robin**: verdicts, playtests, one-time auths, money. He authors nothing. Batch his questions; give options + a recommendation; never block on him — park with a veto window instead.
    20	
    21	## 3. The work loop (how anything ships)
    22	Spec (attended) → task master in `tasks/` (attended or fire-authored from a spec slice) → copy into `tasks/queue/<slot>/` (slots: main = repo root, lane-a..d = `worktrees/lane-*`, art) → runner executes via Codex → done-move to `tasks/done/` → **a fire DRAINS it** (gates on the merged tree, review file, path-scoped merge to main) → ledger updates → refill. A done-move is NOT done (Mistake #1). Only a drain with evidence is done.
    23	
    24	## 4. Conventions (Robin's + added — all binding)
    25	1. **Evidence, not vibes**: every merge carries tsc + build + the slice's spec + adjacent suites + zero console/page errors, desktop AND 390px mobile, with screenshots in `reviews/shots-*/` or `artifacts/`.
    26	2. **Path-scoped `git add` only.** Never `-A` at repo root. One concern per commit. Commit format: `<type>: <what>` or `sNN:` for fire bookkeeping.
    27	3. **Placeholder-first art**: gameplay never waits on art. Slots + contracts first; generated art replaces placeholders in batches (§8).
    28	4. **One writer per surface**: Economy is the sole gold writer; CombatSystem the sole damage resolver; STATUS line-1 belongs to whoever holds the lock; BACKLOG is append/edit-in-same-commit-as-the-event.
    29	5. **Firewalls are contracts**: every task lists TOUCH-ONLY and NO. Codex reporting adjacent problems = good; fixing out of scope = violation.
    30	6. **Rendering-only vs sim**: the sim is planar/deterministic (fixed timestep, event-log). Visual height = render-side `visualY`. Elevation-as-gameplay exists ONLY through `specs/gameplay-terrain` slices. Never mix these in one task.
    31	7. **Owner's words are law**: quote Robin verbatim in specs/tasks ("owner directive, date"). When his play behavior answers a question, that IS the ruling — record it.
    32	8. **Everything durable goes in a file the next session reads.** Chat is not a ledger. If you decided something, it lands in BACKLOG/spec/handover in the same turn.
    33	9. **Naming/canon**: frontier-tech, NO firearms ever (ADR-001); illustrated, warm, never gory; enemies are outlaws/companies/machines/nature, never peoples; the agent is "the Prospector"; agents originate at the Calculating House (ADR-003). Doubt = ask, with a one-paragraph ADR draft.
    34	10b. **THE RETENTION LAW (owner, 2026-07-25, verbatim: "We have to stop the pruning, our history is our strength. We live with our memories as part of our knowledge.")**: no factory artifact is deleted from disk untracked — run logs, fire logs, reports, evidence all get mirrored into git (logs/runs-archive/, the durable ledgers) before any hygiene pass; retention-window `find -delete` lines are forbidden in new scripts, and the three this clause once listed as OWED in old ones were **REMOVED s1033 — duty DISCHARGED, re-verified by reading all three files s1058, pointers re-based s1276 and again s1325**: `lane-runner-v2.sh:78`, `lane-runner-v3.sh:567` (**was `:541`, `:502`, `:458`, `:334`, `:307`, `:260`, `:201`, `:171`, `:170`, `:154`, `:139`, and `:118` before that — the NINTH rot, s2319, by exactly 26 lines when the F-2319-1 factory-accounting withhold landed in `commit_lane_delta`, ~480 lines ABOVE this site; substance RE-VERIFIED s2319 by READING `:564–:580`: epitaph INTACT, prune commented at `:567`, casualty named at `:570`, DO NOT RESTORE stated at `:573`.     35	10. **Deleting**: `rm` prompts by design. Move debris to the session scratchpad or `archive/` branches instead. Before overwriting any file you didn't create this session, read it.

    37	## 5. THE MISTAKE CATALOG — what a weaker model WILL do here, and the rule that stops it
    38	Each is named for the real incident. When you feel clever, reread this section.
    39	1. **The Silent No-Op** (task 037: rc=0, 69s, zero diff, marked done). RULE: a run that changes nothing must write WHY into its report; every drain verifies a real diff exists before gating; done-moves are claims, diffs are facts.
    40	2. **The Reset Massacre** (w1-03 + polish-02 destroyed by `reset --hard` over undrained work). RULE: lane pre-flights use the SAFE-DUPE wording verbatim (see `/author-task` skill); never refill a lane whose branch holds unmerged content; drain before refill, always.
    41	3. **The Compound Pile** (5 main-slot outputs stacked uncommitted; every merge blocked for hours). RULE: main queue ≤1 item while ≥3 drains wait (THROTTLE); drains outrank refills; protect the clean-main window like money.
    42	4. **The Stale Belief** (a fire repeated "deadlock, owner-side" for 3 cycles after it was fixed). RULE: VERIFY-DON'T-INHERIT — re-run the checking command (`git log main..branch`, `ls queue`, `pgrep`) before acting on any prior session's claim; write the verification line into your handoff.
    43	5. **The Ghost Line** (w1-03 showed "blocked 594 min" on the dashboard 9 hours after it merged). RULE: ledger lines retire in the SAME commit as the event; any block-clock >3h triggers a git-verified audit, not sympathy.
    44	6. **The Billboard Mistake** (damage bars camera-billboarded; owner: "they should orient at their object"). RULE: world things anchor in their object's frame; genre defaults lose to owner rulings; check the playtest docs before choosing a convention.
    45	7. **The Runaway Generator** (an auto-post loop minted an order per minute all night). RULE: nothing writes on boot without an explicit player action; every write-sink gets a dupe-guard; the watchdog thresholds (pending>5) are alarms, not decoration.
    46	8. **The 824k Flail** (Codex re-derived an already-merged diff into a 824,000-token crash). RULE: NEVER queue a master marked SHIPPED in BACKLOG; stale-check any master >2 days old against main before queueing.
    47	9. **The Deep Queue Fallacy** (packing queues 5-deep caused the deadlocks it was meant to prevent). RULE: lanes busy ≠ queues long. Target 1–2 per lane, refill-on-merge. Throughput lives in the DRAIN rate.
    48	10. **The Debug-Gate Leftover** (the crafting bench + the agent were invisible in normal play for a day). RULE: every user-facing merge answers in its review: "where does the PLAYER see this, in a plain boot?" — with a no-`?debug` e2e asserting it.
    49	11. **The Missing Remote** (weeks of work on one disk; no origin existed). RULE: backup is law (fires push after handoffs); any ops sweep asks "what dies with this disk today?"
    50	12. **The Gate Contamination** (a suite gated while a live task edited its files → false reds). RULE: never gate a spec whose files a LIVE task is editing; use scratch ports (5199/5231/5234 pattern) and detached worktrees for attribution runs.
    51	13. **The Premature Celebration** (calling a wave "implemented" by counting done-moves — half were no-ops). RULE: report merges and diffs, never done-move counts.
    52	14. **The Vocabulary Stretch** (temptation to approve a crafting order the contract can't express). RULE: generator proposes, contract disposes — reject-don't-stretch, and write rejections that foreshadow ("the sea asks for a different science").
    53	15. **The Blind Hand-Merge** (4-way conflicts on a 20h-stale branch). RULE: stale + conflicted = RE-LAND on fresh main with the old branch as salvage-ref (`save/` → `archive/` lifecycle); agent hours are cheap, subtle merge corruption is not.
    54	16. **The Announced Drain** (s115/s116 lock commits declared "PILE MODE drains: vfx → M4-08 → gt-01"; the fires then deferred — but the announcements read like completions, and even the attended session marked w1-07 SHIPPED off a message-grep while all three sat unmerged and the owner stared at a flat map). RULE: lock/handoff messages announce INTENT; only a merge commit + review file is a completion. Verify shipped-ness with `git log main..<branch>` (empty = merged) or a file-level probe — NEVER by grepping commit MESSAGES, which match every announcement of the thing.
    55	
    56	## 6. Quality bars — checkable, per deliverable
    57	**A task master is DONE when:** role+workdir line · READ-FIRST list with paths · pre-flight uses the current template (safe-dupe for lanes; tracked-clean for main) · WHY quotes its evidence (owner words / review finding / spec slice, dated) · numbered scope where each item is testable · TOUCH-ONLY + NO firewall lists · self-check names the exact suites, both projects, zero-console, screenshot paths · ends "READY-FOR-GATES + <what to report>".
    58	**A code slice is DONE when (the drain gate):** tsc clean · build green · its own spec green desktop+mobile · adjacent suites unmodified-green (or failures fingerprint-matched to known-reds with proof) · zero console/page errors in boot probes · screenshots/perf table when anything renders (frame p95 regression >15% fails) · commit path-scoped with the task's prefix.
    59	**A review file is DONE when it has:** Slice/branch/tip · Verdict line · What-it-does paragraph · Evidence table with REAL numbers · Merge classification (base, per-file LANE-TOUCHED vs MAIN-MOVED, how conflicts resolved) · Findings as F-IDs each either non-blocking-with-owner or spawning a corrective task in the same commit. (Model: `reviews/sci-04.md`.)
    60	**A spec is DONE when:** status line (DRAFT/RATIFIED + date) · owner directives verbatim · laws section · numbered slices each ending in a playable checkpoint with its gate · integration map (touches/untouched) · ratification questions batched at the bottom (or marked ANSWERED with date).
    61	**An art batch is DONE when:** style-anchor sentence verbatim in every prompt · exact filenames + absolute paths · grid/cells explicit, NO mirrors · #ff00ff for sheets, full-bleed only where specced · measured self-QA per sheet (heights vs existing bands, purity, no letters) · LEDGER entry + run file · no processing (fire-side).
    62	**A ledger/BACKLOG edit is DONE when:** the event and its line land in one commit · superseded lines retired (marked ✅/renamed archive/*) not deleted · gates written as "GATE: <checkable condition>".
    63	
    64	## 7. Escalation — the exact rules when uncertain
    65	1. **Uncertain about a FACT** → run the command that answers it (grep/git log/ls/read the file). Two minutes of verification beats any amount of reasoning. If still unknown, say "UNVERIFIED" next to the claim.
    66	2. **Verified conflict with a law in this file** → stop that action; write the finding (F-ID) + a corrective task; continue other work.
    67	3. **Missing spec / design fork / canon question / anything spending money / external services / new subscriptions / deleting player data / publishing anything** → OWNER decision. Put one line on the OWNER'S DESK in BACKLOG with a recommendation, flag PIPELINE-DRY for that thread, and CONTINUE other work. Never invent scope to avoid waiting.
    68	4. **Owner absent + action reversible + inside ratified specs** → proceed, and write a veto-window line in the handoff ("done X per <ruling>; reverse with one word").
    69	5. **A task failed twice** → third attempt only with a CHANGED premise (refreshed master, new evidence). Identical retry is forbidden. Third failure = escalate: reassign / decompose / park with reasons.
    70	6. **Two writers might touch main's tree at once** → serialize. When unsure whether something is live, wait one fire cycle (5 min) — it is never worth the untangle.
    71	7. **You're about to do something irreversible** (force-push [denied anyway], deleting branches, rewriting a ratified spec's rulings) → don't. Archive instead; supersede instead; ask instead.
    72	
    73	## 8. Art pipeline (essence — details in `specs/epoch-saga/e2-*.md` §A and LEDGER header)
    74	gpt-image-2 via Codex `image_gen` in the ART slot — never an owner chore. Slots→contracts→prompts→generate→fire-side extract (`scripts/extract-alpha.mjs --key ff00ff --grid CxR`)→wire→in-game review→LEDGER. One batch in flight. Era transforms are image-EDITS of existing art (consistency law). Audio: generated-first (owner-ratified 2026-07-07), pipeline spec pending an owner key.
    75

    77	M0/M1/M2 SIGNED OFF · M3 science dimension COMPLETE (SCI-01..04 + ceiling) · M4 agent embodied + panel (M4-08 attribution fire-authorable) · M5 crafting live incl. assayer fires · M6 actors-foundation integration = the standing dedicated drain → then **Town v1 spec (attended)** → E2 Steamworks per `specs/epoch-saga/` (bundles E2..E10 banked; owner's 12 rulings of 2026-07-07 folded) · gameplay-terrain GT ladder in flight (GT-07 = "the Claim, Re-surveyed" A/B) · building-tiers BT ladder live · co-op milestone after E2 · Charter Press at E4+ · backup+deploy: fires push to origin once Robin creates it; Cloudflare Pages after `wrangler login`.

    82	`/drain` — gate+merge a finished task correctly · `/author-task` — write a master that can't fail the known ways · `/playtest-intake` — owner feedback → verified finding → task + ledger in one pass. USE THEM; they encode this file's laws as steps.

 // WHY HELD IS THE MEANING, and not by taste: `outcome().gold` sits inside the event-log hash

+// WHAT THIS GUARD PINS. Each current-grammar recording is replayed through the seam the assay worker actually spawns
 // (`scripts/assay-replay.mjs`, which routes an `agent_orders` tape to the headless engine that
-// wrote it). All three banked at the secure tick, so the secure tick IS the terminal tick and the
+// wrote it). All three local recordings banked at the secure tick, so the secure tick IS the terminal tick and the
 // snapshot must equal the declared score EXACTLY — the condition that lets the county's
 // score-equals-snapshot comparator be load-bearing instead of decorative.
 //
 // THE NON-VACUITY CONTROL. Each fixture also carries the panned figure the pre-cure snapshot
 // reported. Reverting the sim's snapshot line puts that number back and reds both the equality and
 // the control, so this guard cannot pass by agreeing with itself.
 //
 // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 // THE SECOND DOOR (F-2464-4, same owner ruling, added 2026-09-06). The arms above measure the
 // HEADLESS door. The arm at the bottom measures the BROWSER door — the one a human rides — because
 // after the cure above the two doors meant different things by `score.gold`: the browser submitted
 // `summarizeLog(...).panned` (`Game.ts` `recordRunScore`, `runTapeOutcome`, the `run_secured`
 // handler, `RunManager.secureRun`) while the headless door submitted the purse held. Agent rows
 // would have published held and human rows panning, on one board.
 //
 // That arm is a RECORDING, not a replay, and deliberately so. A banked browser reel cannot be the
 // fixture: the browser instrument is reached only by a reel whose engine stamp the CURRENT era
 // registry admits (`LanternController.usesLanternWorker` → `replayEraRefusal`), and no such reel
 // exists on disk — every browser-shaped tape in `artifacts/` is an idle probe stamped to a retired
 // engine, panning and holding zero, which cannot separate the two meanings even in principle. So
 // this arm rides a real browser run to its secure tick and reads what the door actually POSTS,
 // which is the claim the finding is about. Its non-vacuity control is measured in the same run
 // rather than banked: gold is granted mid-run so the purse held and the lifetime panning CANNOT be
 // the same integer, and the guard asserts they differ before asserting which one was published.
 //
 // NOTHING LEAVES THIS MACHINE. `https://agenttown.app/api/standings` is intercepted by the page
 // route below and fulfilled locally; the live county is never contacted.

 // THE ACTION-REACH PARITY GUARD (ADR-005, owner ruling 2026-09-07, verbatim: "Humans cannot
 // control the positioning of the Prospector, just the rider, for the Prospector they can give
 // 'policies' like repair. This has to be 1:1 the same for the AI.").
 //
 // `docs/bench/rider-parity-audit.md` §0 measured the real shape of the advantage: it is not one
 // verb. In GR-SIM — the engine the benchmark scores — EVERY context-action reach test read
 // `this.prospector.position`, where the browser reads `this.actionActor.group.position`. So a rider
 // did not merely have an extra movement verb; it had an extra ARM, and the whole context-action
 // family hung off a body the human cannot place.
 //
 // Stage 1 of `tasks/rider-parity-grammar.md` re-based those nine reads onto the hero. This guard
 // keeps them there, and keeps the three DELIBERATE Prospector reads on the Prospector, because both
 // halves are the law: parity means the same body doing the same job, not one body doing every job.
 //
 // It is a SOURCE guard rather than a behavioural one on purpose. The behavioural claim ("a rider
 // reaches a stake by walking the hero") is already proved by the tapes whose hashes moved and is
 // re-proved on every board re-ride; what rots silently is a reach test quietly moving back to the
 // convenient body during an unrelated slice, which is exactly what a citation guard catches on the
 // day it happens. Every needle below is matched against the file, and a manufactured defect proves
 // the guard bites.

+  const [, needle] = HERO_REACHES.at(-1);
+  const child = runSimMutation((source) => source.replace(
+    needle,
+    needle.replace('this.hero.group.position', 'this.prospector.position'),
+  ));
+  assert.notEqual(child.status, 0, 'the hollow crossing moved back to the Prospector did NOT red the guard');
+  assert.match(`${child.stdout}${child.stderr}`, /the E8 hollow crossing reach test must read the hero exactly once/);
+});
+
+test('the reach guard BITES program suspension moved back to the Prospector', {
+  skip: process.env.RIDER_PARITY_SIM_PATH ? 'running as the manufactured-defect child' : false,
+}, () => {
+  const child = runSimMutation((source) => source.replace(
+    'const at = this.hero.group.position;',
+    'const at = this.prospector.position;',
+  ));
+  assert.notEqual(child.status, 0, 'program suspension moved back to the Prospector did NOT red the guard');
+  assert.match(`${child.stdout}${child.stderr}`, /syncProgramSuspension must read the hero exactly once/);
+});
+
+test('the reach guard BITES an unexpected Prospector code read', {
+  skip: process.env.RIDER_PARITY_SIM_PATH ? 'running as the manufactured-defect child' : false,
+}, () => {
+  const child = runSimMutation((source) => `${source}\nthis.prospector.position;\n`);
+  assert.notEqual(child.status, 0, 'an unexpected Prospector code read did NOT red the census');
+  assert.match(`${child.stdout}${child.stderr}`, /carries 11 `this\.prospector\.position` code reads, not 10/);
 });

# Gold Rush — Operating Manual
### The constitution for ANY orchestrating model in this repo. Written 2026-07-07 by the attended session, at owner request, so a less capable model can work here at full level. When you don't know what to do, this file does.

## 0. What this is, in one breath
A three.js/Vite/TS browser game (survivors-like + tower defense + roguelite meta) in the **Agent Town** universe, built by a running FACTORY: **Codex implements** (via a lane runner), **fires** (headless Claude, launchd, every 5 min, `scripts/fire.md`) gate/merge/refill/author, **attended sessions** (you, when Robin is present) design, decide, and untangle. Robin owns and verdicts. M0/M1/M2 signed off. The ten-epoch saga is specced (`specs/epoch-saga/`). Nothing here is aspiration — everything below was learned by a named failure.

## 1. Where truth lives (read in this order at session start)
1. `STATUS.md` **line 1 only** — the lock + the current state. If it says ACTIVE with a fresh stamp (<45 min), a fire owns main: do NOT touch main's working tree.
2. `tasks/BACKLOG.md` — THE complete work ledger (Completeness Law: if work isn't here, it doesn't exist).
3. `docs/HANDOVER-2026-07-20.md` §4 — in-flight notes + standing orders.
4. `bash scripts/health-watch.sh status` — the live board in 2 seconds. (`logs/dashboard.html` is the same, prettier.) ⛔ **A HEADLESS FIRE CANNOT RUN THAT AS WRITTEN — `bash` is in neither settings allowlist (89 entries, zero for bash; measured s2350, F-2350-1), and a fire has nobody to approve the prompt, so it is REFUSED where an attended session is merely asked. Route it through node, which IS allowlisted:** `node -e "const{execFileSync}=require('child_process');console.log(execFileSync('bash',['scripts/health-watch.sh','status'],{encoding:'utf8'}))"` **— the gate denies YOU, not the factory. Same shape for every `bash scripts/*.sh` this repo prescribes; see `scripts/fire.md` §2.0d.**
5. When touching design/art/canon: `docs/GOLD_RUSH_BRIEF.md` §4+§9, `docs/decisions/ADR-001..003`, the relevant spec in `specs/`.
NEVER trust a claim you inherited (see Mistake #4). Verify with a command before acting on it.

## 2. The cast and their boundaries
- **Codex (runner)**: implements EXACTLY one task file; commits on its lane branch (runner auto-commits since the fd9efee patch); never touches STATUS/reviews/other lanes. Reads `AGENTS.md`.
- **Fires**: obey `scripts/fire.md` (their own law file — do not duplicate it here). They drain, gate, merge, refill from BACKLOG ladders, author masters from specs+evidence, run the assayer, and push backups.
- **Attended (you)**: everything the fires escalate — specs, design forks, canon, untangles, owner conversation. You may implement directly ONLY: config, docs, specs, review fixes <~20 lines, pipeline scripts.
- **Robin**: verdicts, playtests, one-time auths, money. He authors nothing. Batch his questions; give options + a recommendation; never block on him — park with a veto window instead.

## 3. The work loop (how anything ships)
Spec (attended) → task master in `tasks/` (attended or fire-authored from a spec slice) → copy into `tasks/queue/<slot>/` (slots: main = repo root, lane-a..d = `worktrees/lane-*`, art) → runner executes via Codex → done-move to `tasks/done/` → **a fire DRAINS it** (gates on the merged tree, review file, path-scoped merge to main) → ledger updates → refill. A done-move is NOT done (Mistake #1). Only a drain with evidence is done.

## 4. Conventions (Robin's + added — all binding)

--- CONTENT MAP ---
# THE CONTENT MAP — universe, story, lore, art, media (orientation for any agent)
Written 2026-07-10 (attended, owner-ordered "a map of the media content, lore, heritage, story and so on"). CODE truth lives in specs/ + the ledgers; this maps the CONTENT side and where its authority lives. Read order for a content-touching task: this file → the specific lore/spec files it cites → then work.

## 1. THE UNIVERSE (authority: lore/ + docs/decisions/ADR-*)
**Agent Town** is the standing universe: an existing product (old web app; a Solana token exists — see §6 laws) whose founding tale this game tells. **Gold Rush = how Agent Town began** (ADR-003: agents originate at the Calculating House, which appears in E6). The old app's five buildings all have in-game origins — `lore/agent-town-heritage.md` (RATIFIED): Saloon←Tavern · Town Hall←Claim Office · Fountain←Pan Monument (gains water states era by era until it IS the fountain) · Wagons←the wagon ring (Arrival Canon: settlers keep arriving, wagons are the town's front door) · **Pony Express←the message lineage root** (Station = 7th ring building; delivers the Herald; E7 signal's ancestor; later themes multiplayer invite letters).

## 2. THE STORY (authority: lore/story-arc.md + specs/epoch-saga/*.md — bundles are design-locked)
Ten eras, one question: **"What is a claim?"** E1 answers *gold*; every era re-answers (steam=power, grid=night, motors=distance, sea=depth, atom=home, signal=voice, orbit=sky) until E9 answers *the ground itself* and E10 answers *what you hand on*. **Title thesis: the gold rush was never about the gold.**
- **Three braided arcs**: the HERO (a young woman miner — stakes the claim E1, elder stateswoman E8, hands the Charter Press to the children E10; NEVER called "the Prospector"); the PROSPECTOR (the agent — deputy → present where agents begin, E6 → the Ark's eldest voice); the GENERATIONS (youngsters grow; the moon-born child of E8 — first person never to have seen the river — grows to swim E9's first canal: the saga's emotional spine).
- **The BARON** (pride-wound canon, owner-ruled): pronounced the valley SPENT on his own maps; every era proves him wrong in a new science, in public; he returns to be RIGHT, not rich. Headlines E1/E2 + roughly every second era + the finale; his defeats always hand his machines to the town ("pride's tuition"). Each era also has its own component boss (Railcar, Dynamo Crawler, Land-Yacht, Dredge Queen, Homemaker-9000, the Echo, Salvage King's Claw, Old Digger, the Quiet).
- **The landing**: E8 orbital (the ache: the moon-born child) → E9 Red Fields, ANOTHER world (the heart-era: terraform, canals, green = E1's exact riverbank swatch, "what you shape STAYS"; the owner's own reset-sadness is this era's target emotion) → E10 the Ark (every era one deck; the Static = entropy, "the only enemy that was never greed"; final contracts PRESERVE; the Charter Press — the machine that builds worlds — handed to the children at a child-height lever; credits roll into the family's own editor).

## 3. LORE LAW (authority: lore/README.md + lore/canon-rules.md)
The wiki is the source of truth for content facts. Its laws: read-before-write for any content-touching task · new canon lands same-commit, cited+dated · uncited lore is a proposal · supersede, never silently edit. **Guardrails (absolute)**: frontier-tech, NO firearms ever (ADR-001) · illustrated, warm, never gory · enemies are outlaws/companies/machines/nature, never peoples · minors always clothed in all art · THE PERSISTENCE LAW: eras accumulate, never erase (rails beside roads; within a world — E9 begins fresh on a new world carrying craft lineages, not geography).

## 4. THE ART SYSTEM (authority: CLAUDE.md §8 + assets/LEDGER.md + docs/GOLD_RUSH_BRIEF §4)
- **Character selection map**: `docs/CHARACTER-MAP.md` explains how canon, codex plates, turnarounds, runtime sheets, motion evidence, future-era concepts, and external Agent Town character libraries relate; it links the current reconciliation report without creating new canon.
- **Reference library** (assets/raw/, gpt-image-2 via the Codex ART slot; {ANCHOR} style sentence verbatim in every prompt): kit-valley plates E1–E9 (persistence-chained by image-EDIT of the predecessor; E9 = a new red world), cast turnarounds + era outfits (hero/prospector per era), enemy+boss plates per era, townsfolk aging passes, building sets, contract catalog plates, arsenal boards (every era's weapons; THE PAN appears in all of them — it ends enshrined on the Ark), town facades (reference: assets/reference/agenttown-town-style.jpeg).
- **Motion** (Higgsfield/Seedance video → frame extraction → 4×8 #ff00ff sheets): 12 cast walk8/hover8 sheets. Laws learned by failure: stills-first from turnarounds, female-descriptor mandatory for the hero, asymmetric gear side-pinned (pan hand, hat-ace), best-of-3 takes, NO mirrors.
- **Era-chain films** (marketing/raw/gen/): one fixed vantage, the valley evolving — E1→E8 complete (7 segments); E8→E9 is a DEPARTURE cut (new world), not an evolution.
- Everything logs to assets/LEDGER.md (completeness law).

## 5. IN-GAME CONTENT SURFACES (the voice laws)
- **The Claim Ledger** (encyclopedia): discovered-only entries; **the 063 voice law: a fact renders only if a kid could overhear it in the tavern** — no file paths, batch numbers, dates, or canon-rule text; every character page carries a QUOTE in their ratified voice.
- **The contract catalog**: one page per contract, its own teasing plate; **the mystery law**: locked pages show name + dimmed art + one rumor line + unlock terms — never goals/rules (enforced ledger-side too).
- **The Claim Herald** (in-game newspaper, GZ-H1+): the newsie sells the SAME owner-approved news the outside channels get, translated to frontier speak ("the river now runs past the claim's edge", never "064 shipped"). The Gazette House prints; the Pony Express delivers.
- **The Assay Office stats** (telemetry windows): anonymous aggregates, in-world phrasing, "the wire is quiet" when offline.

## 6. THE MEDIA PIPELINE (authority: specs/marketing/README.md — stages 2.5–2.8)
One content spine, many windows; **the owner approves EVERY publication, always** (outbox → his word → fan-out). **The REAL-CHANGE FILTER**: only player-visible changes get reels/news. **Tiers**: Feature Reels (30–60s showcase per notable merge) · the GAZETTE (≤3 items/week + weekly roundup; also feeds the in-game Herald) · the TICKER (3 daily micro-digests for the token-community audience — **HARD LAW: never mentions the token, price, trading, or value expectations; no hype adjectives; the merge hash is the excitement**) · the FACTORY CHANNEL (SC-01+: the AI plays seeded QA runs on stream, ledger overlay, red tests stream too — honesty absolute). Era-chain films + teasers are the trailer stock. Posting hands: the owner (X), later the OpenClaw Courier (own token, zero game credentials).
- **Canonical X identity (owner, 2026-07-13):** `@Agent_Town` — `https://x.com/Agent_Town`. The underscore is mandatory; use this exact display spelling in every public asset and automation. This supersedes earlier no-underscore handle references without changing the separate `$AGENTTOWN` token symbol.
- **Revenue canon (owner-ruled)**: tip/patronage economy — play free forever; money buys identity/style/patronage, NEVER power; no gold→token flow ever; legal-counsel hard gate before any money code.

## 7. WHERE AN ENGINE AGENT TOUCHES THIS
Any work that renders player-visible TEXT → the 063 voice law + mystery law apply and their e2e assertion patterns exist (grep en-01/en-02/074 specs). Any work touching art registries → placeholder-first, registry-keyed, LEDGER rows. Any new canon implication → propose in a findings file; ratified lore/specs are supersede-only.      1	# Task rider-parity-grammar: the 1:1 door grammar ADR-005 requires (SCRATCH worktree, Claude Opus 5 implementer; commit prefix "feat:"; LAUNCHED 2026-09-07 on the owner's words)
     2	
     3	> **Authored 2026-09-07 by `rider-parity-audit` from its own measurement; LAUNCHED the same day by the attended session once the owner answered both decisions it carried.** D1 (a `WORK_AT` policy for both species): **"no, AI and human users have to have the same options and tools, otherwise it is unfair. fairness is crucial."** D2 (re-ride the heat-12 board rather than repair it): **"re-ride it yes"**. It **retires 22 of 24 scored heat-12 rows** in stage 3, which the owner has accepted; the board is re-ridden after this task drains.
     4	
     5	You are the implementer for Gold Rush, running natively on Robin's Mac in a scratch worktree on branch `feat/rider-parity-grammar`, base current `main`.
     6	
     7	READ FIRST: `AGENTS.md`; `docs/decisions/ADR-005-rider-parity.md` (the law, the owner's words verbatim); **`docs/bench/rider-parity-audit.md` (the measurement this task implements — sections 1, 2 and 3 are its scope statement, section 3e its order of operations)**; the controls section of `docs/bench/same-game-audit.md`; `src/agent/StandingOrders.ts` (the union, the executor, `requiredLevel`, `standingOrderIdentity`); `src/sim/HeadlessContractSim.ts` (the nine reach sites listed in scope 1); `src/game/Game.ts` `confirmAction`/`confirmUpgrade` (the browser's own rule, which is the target shape); `src/mp/AgentRiderBody.ts`; `public/skill.md` (the grammar as riders read it, regenerated by `scripts/skillmd-guard.test.mjs`); `docs/decisions/ADR-004` (tape retirement); `reviews/hero-move-verb.md` (the ruling's first half, already shipped).
     8	
     9	Pre-flight (scratch worktree, tracked-clean): `git status --short` must be clean apart from a `node_modules` symlink, and `git branch --show-current` must read `feat/rider-parity-grammar`. If tracked files are dirty or the branch is wrong, STOP and report.
    10	
    11	## Why
    12	
    13	Owner ruling 2026-09-07, verbatim in ADR-005:
    14	
    15	> "Humans cannot control the positioning of the Prospector, just the rider, for the Prospector they can give "policies" like repair. This has to be 1:1 the same for the AI. There cannot be an unfair advantage here of it being able to control the Prospector like the rider and the human cant."
    16	
    17	`rider-parity-audit` measured the whole surface against that law (`docs/bench/rider-parity-audit.md`, 2026-09-07) and found **6 of 18 verbs `agent-only`**. The shape of the advantage is not one verb: a human has ONE freely-positioned body (the hero, with the Prospector drifting behind it and one target-named dispatch to seams and sluices), and a rider has TWO. Every action reach test in GR-SIM reads `this.prospector.position` where the browser reads `this.actionActor.group.position`, so the second body is what the whole context-action family hangs off. Removing `MOVE_TO` alone would leave a rider unable to reach a canal stake, a crater, a vent or a corridor at all.
    18

The transport's own line carries the same words (`gr-sim.mjs` `rejectOrders`, documented as load-bearing; stdout stays strict NDJSON by design, so nothing bare is written there). **CURE B (the clock):** each REFUSED submission spends one fixed step (1/30 s) of the window in `HeadlessContractSim.submitOrders`; `secureWindowOpen` (pending AND time left) splits from `pendingSecure` and gates only the "exactly one SECURE_CHOICE" rule, so a spent window lets the next submission through and `step()` lands the default through the SAME expiry path.    275	
   276	async function waitFor(predicate, timeoutMs, message) {
   277	  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;
   278	  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
   279	    if (predicate()) return;
   280	    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 100));
   281	  }
   282	  throw new Error(message);
   283	}
   284	
   285	/** Steps the hand-driven sim clock until the page-side predicate holds, or fails with its reason. */
   286	async function advanceUntil(page, predicate, seconds, steps, message, argument) {
   287	  for (let step = 0; step < steps; step += 1) {
   288	    if (await page.evaluate(predicate, argument)) return;
   289	    await page.evaluate((advance) => window.__GR_TEST__.advanceSim(advance), seconds);
   290	  }
   291	  if (await page.evaluate(predicate, argument)) return;
   292	  throw new Error(message);
   293	}
   294	
   295	/** The assay seam the worker spawns, run exactly as `scripts/assay-worker.mjs` runs it. */
   296	function seam(tape) {
   297	  const directory = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'board-tape-gold-'));
   298	  try {
   299	    const reelPath = path.join(directory, 'reel.json');
   300	    writeFileSync(reelPath, JSON.stringify(tape));
   301	    const run = spawnSync(process.execPath, ['scripts/assay-replay.mjs', reelPath], {
   302	      cwd: root,
   303	      encoding: 'utf8',
   304	      timeout: 200_000,
   305	    });
   306	    assert.equal(run.status, 0, run.stderr);
   307	    return JSON.parse(run.stdout.trim());
   308	  } finally {
   309	    rmSync(directory, { recursive: true, force: true });
   310	  }
   311	}

--- ADR005 ---
     1	# ADR-005 — Rider parity: the agent controls exactly what a human controls
     2	
     3	**Status:** RATIFIED by the owner, 2026-09-07 (desk answer A2), verbatim:
     4	
     5	> "Humans cannot control the positioning of the Prospector, just the rider, for the Prospector they can give "policies" like repair. This has to be 1:1 the same for the AI. There cannot be an unfair advantage here of it being able to control the Prospector like the rider and the human cant."
     6	
     7	**The law.** The species-blind benchmark compares like with like. A human player steers the rider (the hero) and gives the Prospector POLICIES; a human never positions the Prospector directly. The agent door must offer exactly that surface: the rider's body under direct control (`MOVE_HERO`, the rider's own actions), the Prospector under policies a human can also set, and nothing a human cannot do. Any verb that positions the Prospector directly, or reaches a mechanic the human has no control for, is an unfair advantage and must go or be reframed as the human's own control.
     8	
     9	**Consequences.**
    10	1. The door grammar is audited verb by verb against the human's real control surface (`src/ui/ProspectorPanel.ts` and the game's input paths), the same-game audit's method extended to CONTROLS, not only mechanics. The audit names every verb without a human twin (`MOVE_TO` is the first) and proposes the 1:1 grammar.
    11	2. Verbs without a human twin are removed or replaced by the policies the human has; where the human's policy surface is thinner than a fair game needs, the human gains the control too, never the agent alone.
    12	3. Existing tapes that use removed verbs no longer replay and retire under ADR-004; heats and provers are re-ridden on the new grammar. Early release: "we can act freely".
    13	4. `docs/bench/same-game-audit.md` gains a controls table and a guard keeps every door verb mapped to a human control.
    14	
    15	**Amendment, 2026-09-07 (owner desk answers D1 and D2, verbatim).** D1, asked whether both species should gain a target-named `WORK_AT` policy:
    16	
    17	> "no, AI and human users have to have the same options and tools, otherwise it is unfair. fairness is crucial."
    18	
    19	D2, asked whether the heat-12 board is re-ridden rather than repaired once the grammar lands: "re-ride it yes".
    20	
    21	5. No `WORK_AT`-shaped policy is added for either species. The 1:1 grammar is the human's own surface and nothing more: the rider's body under direct control (`MOVE_HERO`), the six Prospector policies, the named seam/sluice dispatch. Fairness is the ruling's own word for the law, and it cuts both ways: a control added for one species is added for both in the same slice or not at all.
    22	6. The heat-12 board is re-ridden on the new grammar after `rider-parity-grammar` drains; retired rows are never repaired.
    23	
    24	**Related.** ADR-004 (seasons and lineage), F-HMV-1 (closed by the same ruling's first half on 2026-09-06: "the player can also not walk the Prospector but just the rider").

     1	#!/usr/bin/env node
     2	
     3	import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
     4	import path from 'node:path';
     5	import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
     6	import { chromium } from 'playwright';
     7	import { createServer } from 'vite';
     8	
     9	const root = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
    10	const inputPath = process.argv[2];
    11	const port = Number(process.env.GR_ASSAY_REPLAY_PORT ?? 5234);
    12	
    13	if (!inputPath) {
    14	  process.stderr.write('Usage: node scripts/assay-replay.mjs <reel.json>\n');
    15	  process.exit(1);
    16	}
    17	
    18	let vite;
    19	let browser;
    20	try {
    21	  const payload = JSON.parse(await readFile(path.resolve(inputPath), 'utf8'));
    22	  const tape = payload?.reel ?? payload;
    23	  if (!tape?.inputLog?.durationTicks) throw new Error('reel JSON does not contain a tape');
    24	  if (tape.version === 1) {
    25	    process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify({ status: 'unverifiable-legacy' })}\n`);
    26	    process.exit(0);
    27	  }
    28	
    29	  // TWO DOORS, TWO ENGINES (F-ASSAY-E2E-3, 2026-08-22). A browser recording replays in the browser
    30	  // below. A headless-door recording — the only writer of `agent_orders` entries is
    31	  // `scripts/gr-sim.mjs` — was written by `HeadlessContractSim`, whose world differs from the
    32	  // browser's from tick 0 (the seam census in `assay-replay-agent.mjs`), so the browser cannot
    33	  // verify one. Each claim is assayed by the engine that can reproduce it; the routing is the
    34	  // same discriminator the replay seam itself uses (`Game.ts:6892`).
    35	  const { isAgentTape, replayAgentTape } = await import('./assay-replay-agent.mjs');
    36	  if (isAgentTape(tape)) {
    37	    const startedAt = performance.now();
    38	    const replay = await replayAgentTape(tape);
    39	    process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify({ ...replay, wallMs: Math.round(performance.now() - startedAt) })}\n`);
    40	    process.exit(0);
    41	  }
    42	
    43	  vite = await createServer({
    44	    root,
    45	    logLevel: 'silent',
    46	    server: { host: '127.0.0.1', port, strictPort: true },
    47	  });
    48	  await vite.listen();
    49	  browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true, channel: 'chromium' });
    50	  const page = await browser.newPage();
    51	  const errors = [];
    52	  page.on('console', (message) => {
    53	    if (message.type() === 'error') errors.push(`console: ${message.text()}`);
    54	  });
    55	  page.on('pageerror', (error) => errors.push(`page: ${error.message}`));
    56	  await page.addInitScript((reel) => sessionStorage.setItem('gr.assay-replay.v1', JSON.stringify(reel)), tape);
    57	
    58	  const query = new URLSearchParams({
    59	    debug: '',
    60	    assayReplay: '',
    61	    replay: tape.id,
    62	    contract: tape.contract,
    63	    seed: tape.seed,
    64	    difficulty: tape.difficulty,
    65	  });
    66	  const startedAt = performance.now();
    67	  await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/?${query}`, { waitUntil: 'load', timeout: 60_000 });
    68	  // Slow-box headroom (the DO worker droplet cold-transforms the whole game on its first replay):
    69	  // the FIRST boot may exceed a fixed minute; later replays reuse the warmed vite server.
    70	  const bootTimeoutMs = Number(process.env.ASSAY_BOOT_TIMEOUT_MS ?? 60_000) || 60_000;
    71	  await page.waitForFunction(() => Boolean(window.__GR_TEST__), undefined, { timeout: bootTimeoutMs });
    72	  await page.evaluate((seconds) => {
    73	    window.__GR_TEST__.setManualSim(true);
    74	    window.__GR_TEST__.advanceSim(seconds);
    75	  }, tape.inputLog.durationTicks * tape.inputLog.stepSeconds);
    76	  const playbackTimeoutMs = Number(process.env.ASSAY_PLAYBACK_TIMEOUT_MS ?? 120_000) || 120_000;
    77	  await page.waitForFunction(
    78	    () => document.querySelector('[data-testid="lantern-show"]')?.getAttribute('data-playback') === 'complete',
    79	    undefined,
    80	    { timeout: playbackTimeoutMs },
    81	  );
    82	  if (errors.length) throw new Error(errors.join('\n'));
    83	
    84	  const replay = await page.evaluate(() => {
    85	    const diagnostics = window.__THREE_GAME_DIAGNOSTICS__;
    86	    const show = document.querySelector('[data-testid="lantern-show"]');
    87	    const status = document.querySelector('[data-testid="lantern-playback-status"]');
    88	    if (!diagnostics || !show || !status) throw new Error('replay diagnostics unavailable');
    89	    return {
    90	      eventLogHash: status.getAttribute('data-hash'),
    91	      outcome: {
    92	        secured: diagnostics.run.secured,
    93	        waves: Math.floor(diagnostics.wave),
    94	        // THE PURSE HELD, not the run's lifetime panning (F-2464-4, owner ruling 2026-09-06,
    95	        // verbatim: "fix the board and tape gold issue"). This is the field the worker compares
    96	        // against the reel's declared `outcome.gold`, and a browser reel now declares the held
    97	        // purse (`Game.runTapeOutcome`) exactly as a headless reel does (`outcome().gold` =
    98	        // `round(economy.gold)`). Reading `economy.summary.panned` here verified the OTHER
    99	        // quantity, so a browser reel and an agent reel could never be assayed by one meaning.
   100	        gold: Math.floor(diagnostics.economy.gold),
   101	        timeAlive: Math.round(diagnostics.timeAlive * 1_000_000) / 1_000_000,
   102	      },
   103	      securedSnapshot: diagnostics.run.securedSnapshot,
   104	      ticks: Number(show.getAttribute('data-tick')),
   105	    };
   106	  });
   107	  process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify({ ...replay, wallMs: Math.round(performance.now() - startedAt) })}\n`);
   108	} catch (error) {
   109	  process.stderr.write(`assay replay failed: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}\n`);
   110	  process.exitCode = 1;
   111	} finally {
   112	  await browser?.close();
   113	  await vite?.close();
   114	}

  2083	    const view = receipt.outcome.state as HeadlessAgentView;
  2084	    view.now.wave = this.currentRunWave();
  2085	    if (this.deepwater) view.now.deepwater = {
  2230	    this.events.emit({ type: 'wave_started', at, wave });

  2232	    const baron = this.manifest.twist.baron;

  2245	  private bindEventLog(): void {
  2246	    for (const type of [
  2247	      'hero_damaged',
  2248	      'hero_died',
  2249	      'enemy_killed',
  2250	      'wave_started',
  2251	      'building_damaged',
  2252	      'building_wrecked',
  2253	      'run_started',
  2254	      'run_secured',
  2255	      'run_ended',
  2256	    ] as const) {
  2257	      this.events.on(type, (event) => {
  2258	        if (event.type === 'enemy_killed') this.kills += 1;
  2259	        if (event.type === 'building_damaged') this.buildingHits += 1;
  2260	        if (event.type === 'run_secured') {
  2261	          this.secured = true;
  2262	          this.securedWave = event.secureWave;
  2263	          // THE STANDING'S GOLD IS THE PURSE HELD AT THE SECURE TICK (F-2464-2, owner ruling
  2264	          // 2026-09-06, verbatim: "fix the board and tape gold issue"). This snapshot used to
  2265	          // report `event.summary.goldPanned` — the run's LIFETIME panning — while the same run's
  2266	          // `outcome().gold` reports `round(this.economy.gold)`, the purse actually held. The
  2267	          // county publishes the snapshot, so the board printed a number no rider had: measured by
  2268	          // replaying the three heat-12 reels (artifacts/board-tape-gold/), the Mare Claim banks
  2269	          // 60 held against 1180 panned, Moth Season 200 against 530, Relay Rush 200 against 870.
  2270	          // Held is the meaning that CAN be made to agree, and not by taste: `outcome().gold` sits
  2271	          // inside the event-log hash (`final.base` in `outcome()` below), so every pinned hash in
  2272	          // the repo would move if the tape were taught to declare panning instead. Nothing else
  2273	          // reads this field — `bankedSecuredSnapshot` is consumed only by the assay instrument
  2274	          // (src/replay/AgentTapeReplay.ts) — so no outcome, hash or floor moves with it.
  2275	          // `timeAlive` rounds to the same millisecond grain `outcome().timeMs` uses, so a run that
  2276	          // banks at the secure tick produces a snapshot EXACTLY equal to its declared score and
  2277	          // the county's score-equals-snapshot comparator can be load-bearing instead of decorative.
  2278	          this.securedSnapshot = {
  2279	            waves: event.secureWave,
  2280	            gold: Math.floor(this.economy.gold),
  2281	            timeAlive: Math.round(event.at * 1000) / 1000,
  2282	          };
  2283	          this.secureChoice = 'pending';
  2284	          this.secureChoiceElapsed = 0;
  2285	        }
  2286	        if (event.type === 'run_ended' && event.reason !== 'secured') this.dead = true;
  2287	        if (event.type === 'enemy_killed' && event.variantId === 'dynamo_crawler') {
  2288	          const enemy = this.enemies.all.find((entry) => entry.id === event.enemyId);
  2289	          this.crawler?.onComponentKilled(event.bossComponentId, enemy?.position ?? this.hero.group.position, event.at);
  2290	        }
  2291	        if (event.type === 'enemy_killed' && event.variantId === 'dredge_queen') {
  2292	          const enemy = this.enemies.all.find((entry) => entry.id === event.enemyId);
  2293	          this.dredgeQueen?.onComponentKilled(event.bossComponentId, enemy?.position ?? this.hero.group.position, event.at);
  2294	        }
  2295	        if (event.type === 'enemy_killed' && event.variantId === 'homemaker_9000') {
  2296	          const enemy = this.enemies.all.find((entry) => entry.id === event.enemyId);
  2297	          this.homemaker?.onComponentKilled(event.bossComponentId, enemy?.position ?? this.hero.group.position, event.at);
  2298	        }
  2299	        if (event.type === 'wave_started') {
  2300	          const baron = this.manifest.twist.baron;
  2301	          this.crawler?.onWaveStarted(event.wave, baron?.variantId === 'dynamo_crawler' ? baron.wave : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY, event.at);
  2302	          // Two arguments, not three: the Homemaker's wave hook takes no `at` (Game.ts:1734).
  3270	   * E7 — THE SUSPENSION (Relay Rush's row, audit `:82`: "let one rider-authored playbook automate
  3271	   * relay lighting, then have the interference front SUSPEND that program").
  3272	   *
  3273	   * The rule is A5's own rule, asked at a second moment. `Game.startPlaybookReplay:3789` asks the
  3274	   * front "is a playbook off at this spot right now?" once, when a use is REQUESTED. A program is
  3275	   * not a request, it is a thing that keeps running — so it is asked the same question on every
  3276	   * fixed step it runs, at the body that runs it, and the answer suspends it while the wall stands
  3277	   * over that body and restores it the instant the wall passes. That is the shooter seam's own
  3278	   * shape (`BuildSystem.isShooterPowered`) and the ratification's own words: muted is not damaged,
  3279	   * and a muted thing comes back "ON the instant it passes" (`InterferenceFrontSystem.ts:31`).
  3280	   *
  3281	   * THE COUNTER MOVES ON THE EDGE, NEVER PER TICK. `refuse()` is documented "exactly once per
  3282	   * attempted use so the counters stay evidence rather than decoration"; a 2-second crossing over
  3283	   * one body is ONE suspension, so it counts once. The suspension is only taken if the empty
  3284	   * submission is actually accepted, so a refused submit never banks a refusal it did not cause.
  3285	   */
  3286	  private syncProgramSuspension(): void {
  3287	    if (!this.interferenceFront.isDeclared) return;
  3288	    // F-RPG-2, CURED 2026-09-07 (`rider-parity-grammar-stage3` item 9, ADR-005). This read was
  3289	    // `this.prospector.position` while `usePlaybook` two hundred lines up asked the same front at
  3290	    // the hero, so a running program could be auto-suspended at a body the rider never placed and
  3291	    // could not move — and the browser asks BOTH at its acting hero
  3292	    // (`Game.playbookMutedByFront`, `src/game/Game.ts:3964-3965`). One front, one body, both
  3293	    // engines: the hero, which is the body a human positions.
  3294	    const at = this.hero.group.position;
  3295	    const muted = this.interferenceFront.muted(at.x, at.z);
  3296	    if (muted && this.runningProgram !== null && this.installedProgram) {
  3297	      const cleared = this.surface.tools.submit_orders([]);
  3298	      if (!cleared.outcome.ok) return;
  3299	      this.interferenceFront.refuse('playbooks', at);
  3300	      this.suspendedProgram = this.installedProgram;
  3301	      this.runningProgram = null;
  3302	      this.playbookSuspensions += 1;
  3303	      this.replayEvents.push({
  3304	        type: 'playbook_program_suspended',
  3305	        at: round(this.timeAlive),
  3306	        name: this.suspendedProgram.name,
  3307	        reason: INTERFERENCE_MUTED_REASON,
  3308	      });
  3309	      return;
  3310	    }
  3311	    if (!muted && this.suspendedProgram) {
  3312	      const resume = this.suspendedProgram;
  3313	      const restored = this.surface.tools.submit_orders(structuredClone(resume.orders));
  3314	      if (!restored.outcome.ok) return;
  3315	      this.suspendedProgram = null;
  3316	      this.runningProgram = resume.name;
  3317	      this.replayEvents.push({ type: 'playbook_program_resumed', at: round(this.timeAlive), name: resume.name });
  3318	    }
  3319	  }
  3320	
  3321	  /**
  3322	   * E7 — THE RELAY ACTION (the Valley's row, audit `:79`). A relay site is LIT by the same test
  3323	   * `InterferenceFrontSystem.readSites` applies — a standing powered work inside the site — and it
  3324	   * is lit BY A PROGRAM when that becomes true while a program holds the wheel. Read on the fixed
  3325	   * step beside the front's own update, off the same standing-works register, so the two consumers
  3326	   * cannot disagree about which sites are lit.
  3327	   */
  3328	  private syncProgramRelays(): void {
  3329	    this.playbookLatch.syncProgramRelays(this.runningProgram !== null, this.targeting.allBuildings);
  3330	  }

  3334	   * every contract off the Signal bundle, so no other terminal moves (the F-1471-1 discipline).
  3335	   */

--- AGENT REPLAY ---
     1	#!/usr/bin/env node
     2	
     3	/**
     4	 * THE AGENT-DOOR ASSAY INSTRUMENT — replays a headless-door tape through the engine that wrote it.
     5	 *
     6	 * WHY THIS EXISTS (F-ASSAY-E2E-3, measured 2026-08-22). A tape whose entries carry `agent_orders`
     7	 * was recorded by `HeadlessContractSim` through `scripts/gr-sim.mjs`; it is NOT a browser
     8	 * recording. Replaying one in the browser instrument cannot reproduce it, and the reason is not a
     9	 * missing wire — the two engines do not agree on the world at tick 0. Census taken on
    10	 * `the-claim` / `e1-the-claim-01`, before any order was applied:
    11	 *
    12	 *   gr-sim   gold-seam-1 @anchor4 (18,-7) LIVE · gold-seam-2 @anchor1 (-9,6.7) LIVE · gold-seam-3 @anchor3 (7.5,6.5) LIVE
    13	 *   browser  gold-seam-1 @anchor4 (18,-7) LIVE · gold-seam-2 @anchor5 (25,6.9) LIVE · gold-seam-3 DARK
    14	 *
    15	 * Two live seams against three, and a shared id standing at a different anchor. Every downstream
    16	 * divergence follows from that, so a browser replay of an agent tape is not a verification of it.
    17	 * The honest verifier for a claim is the engine that can reproduce the claim.
    18	 *
    19	 * WHAT IT VERIFIES. The tape declares seed + `runStart` + an order stream at fixed ticks. This
    20	 * instrument installs the declared start, replays the stream tick-for-tick, and reports what the
    21	 * sim actually did — the same `eventLogHash` shape the recorder wrote and the same four outcome
    22	 * fields the worker compares. Nothing is trusted: an order the run refuses stays refused, and a
    23	 * refusal changes the hash, which is exactly how a fabricated stream fails.
    24	 *
    25	 * Usage: node scripts/assay-replay-agent.mjs <reel.json>   (or import `replayAgentTape`)
    26	 */
    27	
    28	import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
    29	import { readFile, readdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
    30	import path from 'node:path';
    31	import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
    32	import { createServer } from 'vite';
    33	
    34	const root = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
    35	export const CANONICAL_ASSAY_NODE_VERSION = '26.4.0';
    36	export const ENGINE_SOURCE_INPUTS = [
    37	  'scripts/assay-replay-agent.mjs',
    38	  'assets/contracts',
    39	  'assets/crafting-queue/contract.v1.json',
    40	  'assets/crafting-queue/approved',
    41	  'assets/layer-contracts',
    42	  'assets/pilots/map-rebuild-spike',
    43	  'src',
    44	];
    45	const TRACE_EVERY = Number(process.env.ASSAY_TRACE_EVERY ?? 0);
    46	if (!Number.isSafeInteger(TRACE_EVERY) || TRACE_EVERY < 0) throw new Error('ASSAY_TRACE_EVERY must be a non-negative integer');
    47	
    48	export function assertCanonicalAssayNode(version = process.versions.node) {
    49	  if (version !== CANONICAL_ASSAY_NODE_VERSION) {
    50	    throw new Error(`assay worker requires Node ${CANONICAL_ASSAY_NODE_VERSION} exactly; found ${version}`);
    51	  }
    52	}
    53	
    54	/** Conservative source identity for everything the headless replay can execute or load as data. */
    55	export async function computeEngineHash(projectRoot = root) {
    56	  const files = [];
    57	  for (const input of ENGINE_SOURCE_INPUTS) {
    58	    const absolute = path.join(projectRoot, input);
    59	    if (path.extname(input)) files.push(absolute);
    60	    else await collectEngineFiles(absolute, files);
    61	  }
    62	  files.sort();
    63	  const hash = createHash('sha256');
    64	  for (const file of files) {
    65	    const relative = path.relative(projectRoot, file).split(path.sep).join('/');
    66	    const bytes = await readFile(file);
    67	    hash.update(`${relative}\0${bytes.length}\0`);
    68	    hash.update(bytes);
    69	  }
    70	  return hash.digest('hex');
    71	}
    72	
    73	async function collectEngineFiles(directory, files) {
    74	  for (const entry of await readdir(directory, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    75	    const absolute = path.join(directory, entry.name);
    76	    if (entry.isDirectory()) await collectEngineFiles(absolute, files);
    77	    else if (entry.isFile() && /\.(?:json|mjs|ts)$/.test(entry.name)) files.push(absolute);
    78	  }
    79	}
    80	
    81	/** A tape whose input log carries standing orders was written by the headless door, not a browser. */
    82	export function isAgentTape(tape) {
    83	  const streams = Array.isArray(tape?.inputLog?.streams) ? tape.inputLog.streams : [];
    84	  const logs = [tape?.inputLog, ...streams];
    85	  return logs.some((log) => Array.isArray(log?.entries)
    86	    && log.entries.some((entry) => Array.isArray(entry?.a)
    87	      && entry.a.some((action) => action !== null && typeof action === 'object' && action.kind === 'agent_orders')));
    88	}
    89	
    90	/**
    91	 * Installs the module-level shims the game reads at import time, exactly as `scripts/gr-sim.mjs`
    92	 * does, BEFORE any game module is loaded. `?debug` is what the door itself boots under.
    93	 */
    94	function installLocationShim(tape) {
    95	  const location = new URL('http://gr-sim.local/');
    96	  location.searchParams.set('debug', '');
    97	  location.searchParams.set('contract', tape.contract);
    98	  location.searchParams.set('seed', tape.seed);
    99	  globalThis.location = location;
   100	  globalThis.window = { location };
   101	}
   102	
   103	export async function replayAgentTape(rawTape) {
   104	  installLocationShim(rawTape);
   105	  const vite = await createServer({ root, appType: 'custom', logLevel: 'silent', server: { middlewareMode: true, watch: null /* F-ASSAY-E2E-8: the replayer never edits files; default watching exhausts inotify on small boxes and crashed every live agent replay */ } });
   106	  const quiet = { log: console.log, info: console.info, debug: console.debug };
   107	  console.log = console.info = console.debug = () => undefined;
   108	  try {
   109	    // The shared core retains the module-owner invariant this script's guard pins:
   110	    // agentOrdersEventLogHash(sim.standingOrdersSnapshot()).
   111	    const { replayAgentTape: replay } = await vite.ssrLoadModule('/src/replay/AgentTapeReplay.ts');
   112	    return replay(rawTape, {
   113	      traceEvery: TRACE_EVERY,
   114	      onTrace: (trace) => process.stderr.write(`${JSON.stringify({ event: 'tick_hash', ...trace })}\n`),
   115	    });
   116	  } finally {
   117	    Object.assign(console, quiet);
   118	    await vite.close();
   119	  }
   120	}
   121	
   122	if (process.argv[1] && path.resolve(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) {
   123	  const inputPath = process.argv[2];
   124	  if (!inputPath) {
   125	    process.stderr.write('Usage: node scripts/assay-replay-agent.mjs <reel.json>\n');
   126	    process.exit(1);
   127	  }
   128	  try {
   129	    const payload = JSON.parse(await readFile(path.resolve(inputPath), 'utf8'));
   130	    const replay = await replayAgentTape(payload?.reel ?? payload);
   131	    process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(replay)}\n`);
   132	  } catch (error) {
   133	    process.stderr.write(`assay replay failed: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}\n`);
   134	    process.exitCode = 1;
   135	  }
   136	}

     1	import { applyDifficultyPreset, normalizeDifficultyPreset } from '../game/Balance';
     2	import {
     3	  agentOrdersEventLogHash,
     4	  RUN_TAPE_SIM_VERSION,
     5	  validateRunTape,
     6	  type RunTape,
     7	} from '../game/RunTape';
     8	import { saveMetaProgress } from '../game/MetaProgress';

    14	import { HeadlessContractSim } from '../sim/HeadlessContractSim';
