    16	const FROZEN_STEP_ALLOWANCE = 18_000;
    17	
    18	export type AgentTapeReplayResult = {
    19	  eventLogHash: string;
    20	  outcome: { secured: boolean; waves: number; gold: number; timeAlive: number };
    21	  securedSnapshot?: { waves: number; gold: number; timeAlive: number };
    22	  ticks: number;
    23	  engine: 'headless-contract-sim';
    24	};
    25	
    26	export type AgentTapeReplaySnapshot = {
    27	  contract: { id: string; tileId: string; width: number; height: number };
    28	  tick: number;
    29	  wave: number;
    30	  timeAlive: number;
    31	  gold: number;
    32	  hero: { x: number; z: number; hp: number; maxHp: number; alive: boolean };
    33	  rider: { x: number; z: number } | null;
    34	  enemies: Array<{ id: number; kind: string; x: number; z: number; hp: number; maxHp: number; alive: boolean }>;
    35	  works: Array<{ id: string; index: number; x: number; z: number; hp: number; maxHp: number; wrecked: boolean }>;
    36	  seams: Array<{ id: string; x: number; z: number; remaining: number }>;
    37	  pickups: Array<{ index: number; x: number; z: number; amount: number }>;
    38	};
    39	
    40	export type AgentTapeReplayOptions = {
    41	  traceEvery?: number;
    42	  onTrace?: (trace: { step: number; tick: number; hash: string }) => void;
    43	};
    44	
    45	export async function replayAgentTape(rawTape: unknown, options: AgentTapeReplayOptions = {}): Promise<AgentTapeReplayResult> {
    46	  const replay = new AgentTapeReplaySession(rawTape);
    47	  let steps = 0;
    48	  while (!replay.complete && steps < replay.durationTicks + FROZEN_STEP_ALLOWANCE) {
    49	    if (options.traceEvery && steps % options.traceEvery === 0) {
    50	      options.onTrace?.({ step: steps, tick: replay.tick, hash: replay.tickHash(steps) });
    51	    }
    52	    replay.advanceOneTick();
    53	    steps += 1;
    54	  }
    55	  return replay.result();
    56	}
    57	
    58	export class AgentTapeReplaySession {
    59	  readonly durationTicks: number;
    60	  private readonly sim: HeadlessContractSim;
    61	  private readonly orders: Map<number, unknown[]>;
    62	  private readonly applied = new Set<number>();
    63	  private readonly recentEnemies = new Map<number, AgentTapeReplaySnapshot['enemies'][number] & { lastSeen: number }>();
    64	  private steps = 0;
    65	
    66	  constructor(rawTape: unknown) {
    67	    const tape = validateRunTape(withoutEngineHash(rawTape));
    68	    if (!tape) throw new Error('malformed tape');
    69	    if (tape.simVersion !== RUN_TAPE_SIM_VERSION) throw new Error('sim version mismatch');
    70	    if (!tape.runStart) throw new Error('legacy tape v1 is unverifiable');
    71	    if (tape.inputLog.streams.length > 0) throw new Error('an agent tape carries no additional streams');
    72	
    73	    applyDifficultyPreset(normalizeDifficultyPreset(tape.difficulty));
    74	    stageReplayContract(tape.contract);
    75	    this.durationTicks = tape.inputLog.durationTicks;
    76	    this.sim = bootDeclaredRun(tape);
    77	    this.orders = ordersByTick(tape);
    78	  }
    79	
    80	  get tick(): number { return this.sim.replayTick; }
    81	  get complete(): boolean { return this.sim.isTerminal; }
    82	  tickHash(step = this.steps): string { return this.sim.tickHash(step); }
    83	
    84	  advanceOneTick(): void {
    85	    if (this.complete) return;
    86	    const tick = this.tick;
    87	    if (!this.applied.has(tick)) {
    88	      for (const order of this.orders.get(tick) ?? []) this.sim.submitOrders(order);
    89	      this.applied.add(tick);
    90	    }
    91	    this.sim.advanceOneTick();
    92	    this.steps += 1;
    93	  }
    94	
    95	  advanceTo(targetTick: number, stopAfterWave?: number): AgentTapeReplaySnapshot {
    96	    const ceiling = this.durationTicks + FROZEN_STEP_ALLOWANCE;
    97	    while (!this.complete && this.tick < targetTick && this.steps < ceiling) {
    98	      this.advanceOneTick();
    99	      if (stopAfterWave !== undefined && this.snapshot().wave >= stopAfterWave) break;
   100	    }
   101	    return this.snapshot();
   102	  }
   103	
   104	  resumeAt(targetTick: number): HeadlessContractSim {
   105	    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(targetTick) || targetTick < 0 || targetTick > this.durationTicks) {
   106	      throw new Error(`resume tick must be an integer between 0 and ${this.durationTicks}`);
   107	    }
   108	    this.advanceTo(targetTick);
   109	    if (this.tick !== targetTick) throw new Error(`tape ended at tick ${this.tick} before resume tick ${targetTick}`);
   110	    return this.sim;
   111	  }
   112	
   113	  snapshot(): AgentTapeReplaySnapshot {
   114	    const internal = this.sim as unknown as {
   115	      hero: { group: { position: { x: number; z: number } }; hp: number; maxHp: number };
   116	      prospector: { snapshot: { position: { x: number; z: number } } };
   117	      enemies: { all: Array<{
   118	        id: number; isAlive: boolean; currentHp: number; maxHp: number; position: { x: number; z: number };
   119	        variantId: string | null; variantLabel: string | null; eliteKind: string | null; isThief: boolean; isWrecker: boolean;
   120	      }> };
   121	      build: { diagnostics: { hp: AgentTapeReplaySnapshot['works'] extends Array<infer T> ? Array<T & { position: { x: number; z: number } }> : never } };
   122	      harvest: { snapshot: { activeNodes: Array<{ id: string; active: boolean; position: { x: number; z: number }; remaining: number }> } };
   123	      goldPickups: { snapshot(): Array<{ active: boolean; amount: number; position: { x: number; z: number } }> } | null;
   124	      economy: { gold: number };
   125	      waves: { diagnostics: { wave: number } };
   126	      timeAlive: number;
   127	    };
   128	    const aliveIds = new Set<number>();
   129	    for (const enemy of internal.enemies.all) {
   130	      if (!enemy.isAlive) continue;
   131	      aliveIds.add(enemy.id);
   132	      this.recentEnemies.set(enemy.id, {
   133	        id: enemy.id,
   134	        kind: enemy.variantLabel ?? enemy.variantId ?? enemy.eliteKind ?? (enemy.isWrecker ? 'wrecker' : enemy.isThief ? 'thief' : 'claim_jumper'),
   135	        x: enemy.position.x,
   136	        z: enemy.position.z,
   137	        hp: enemy.currentHp,
   138	        maxHp: enemy.maxHp,
   139	        alive: true,
   140	        lastSeen: this.tick,
   141	      });
   142	    }
   143	    for (const [id, enemy] of this.recentEnemies) {
   144	      if (!aliveIds.has(id) && enemy.alive) this.recentEnemies.set(id, { ...enemy, hp: 0, alive: false });
   145	      if (!aliveIds.has(id) && this.tick - enemy.lastSeen > 15) this.recentEnemies.delete(id);
   146	    }
   147	    return {
   148	      contract: {
   149	        id: this.sim.manifest.id,
   150	        tileId: this.sim.manifest.tileParams.tileId,
   151	        width: this.sim.manifest.tileParams.dimensions?.width ?? this.sim.manifest.tileParams.size ?? 64,
   152	        height: this.sim.manifest.tileParams.dimensions?.height ?? this.sim.manifest.tileParams.size ?? 64,
   153	      },
   154	      tick: this.tick,
   155	      wave: internal.waves.diagnostics.wave,
   156	      timeAlive: internal.timeAlive,
   157	      gold: internal.economy.gold,
   158	      hero: {
   159	        x: internal.hero.group.position.x,
   160	        z: internal.hero.group.position.z,
   161	        hp: internal.hero.hp,
   162	        maxHp: internal.hero.maxHp,
   163	        alive: internal.hero.hp > 0,
   164	      },
   165	      rider: internal.prospector?.snapshot?.position
   166	        ? { x: internal.prospector.snapshot.position.x, z: internal.prospector.snapshot.position.z }
   167	        : null,
   168	      enemies: [...this.recentEnemies.values()].map(({ lastSeen: _lastSeen, ...enemy }) => enemy),
   169	      works: internal.build.diagnostics.hp.map((work) => ({
   170	        id: work.id,
   171	        index: work.index,
   172	        x: work.position.x,
   173	        z: work.position.z,
   174	        hp: work.hp,
   175	        maxHp: work.maxHp,
   176	        wrecked: work.wrecked,
   177	      })),
   178	      seams: internal.harvest.snapshot.activeNodes
   179	        .filter((node) => node.active)
   180	        .map((node) => ({ id: node.id, x: node.position.x, z: node.position.z, remaining: node.remaining })),
   181	      pickups: (internal.goldPickups?.snapshot() ?? []).flatMap((pickup, index) => pickup.active
   182	        ? [{ index, x: pickup.position.x, z: pickup.position.z, amount: pickup.amount }]
   183	        : []),
   184	    };
   185	  }
   186	
   187	  result(): AgentTapeReplayResult {
   188	    if (!this.complete) throw new Error(`tape ran out after ${this.steps} steps with the run still alive`);
   189	    const unreached = [...this.orders.keys()].filter((tick) => !this.applied.has(tick));
   190	    if (unreached.length) throw new Error(`the run ended before tick ${unreached[0]} of the order stream`);
   191	    const outcome = this.sim.outcome();
   192	    const securedSnapshot = this.sim.bankedSecuredSnapshot;
   193	    return {
   194	      eventLogHash: agentOrdersEventLogHash(this.sim.standingOrdersSnapshot()),
   195	      outcome: {
   196	        secured: outcome.secured,
   197	        waves: outcome.waves,
   198	        gold: outcome.gold,
   199	        timeAlive: outcome.timeMs / 1000,
   200	      },
   201	      ...(securedSnapshot ? { securedSnapshot } : {}),
   202	      ticks: this.steps,
   203	      engine: 'headless-contract-sim',
   204	    };
   205	  }
   206	}
   207	
   208	function withoutEngineHash(tape: unknown): unknown {
   209	  if (!tape || typeof tape !== 'object' || Array.isArray(tape)) return tape;
   210	  const meta = Reflect.get(tape, 'meta');
   211	  const keys = meta && typeof meta === 'object' && !Array.isArray(meta)
   212	    ? Object.keys(meta).sort().join(',')
   213	    : '';
   214	  if (!meta || typeof meta !== 'object' || Array.isArray(meta)
   215	    || (keys !== 'buildId,engineHash' && keys !== 'buildId,engineHash,era' && keys !== 'buildId,engineHash,era,viewVersion')
   216	    || typeof Reflect.get(meta, 'engineHash') !== 'string'
   217	    || !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(Reflect.get(meta, 'engineHash') as string)) return tape;
   218	  return { ...tape, meta: {
   219	    buildId: Reflect.get(meta, 'buildId'),
   220	    ...(Reflect.has(meta, 'viewVersion') ? { viewVersion: Reflect.get(meta, 'viewVersion') } : {}),
   221	  } };
   222	}
   223	
   224	function bootDeclaredRun(tape: RunTape): HeadlessContractSim {
   225	  const boot = { contractId: tape.contract, seed: tape.seed };
   226	  const declared = stableHash(tape.runStart);
   227	  const virgin = new HeadlessContractSim(boot);
   228	  if (stableHash(virgin.runStart) === declared) return virgin;
   229	
   230	  const storage = new FakeStorage();
   231	  saveMetaProgress(storage, tape.runStart!.meta);
   232	  saveResearchState(storage, tape.runStart!.research);
   233	  const progressed = new HeadlessContractSim(boot, { storage });
   234	  if (stableHash(progressed.runStart) !== declared) {
   235	    throw new Error('declared runStart is not installable by this door');
   236	  }
   237	  return progressed;
   238	}
   239	
   240	function ordersByTick(tape: RunTape): Map<number, unknown[]> {
   241	  const byTick = new Map<number, unknown[]>();
   242	  for (const entry of tape.inputLog.entries) {
   243	    const orders = [];
   244	    for (const action of entry.a) {
   245	      if (!isAgentOrdersAction(action)) throw new Error(`agent tape carries a non-order action: ${'type' in action ? action.type : 'unknown'}`);
   246	      orders.push(action.orders);
   247	    }
   248	    if (orders.length) byTick.set(entry.t, [...(byTick.get(entry.t) ?? []), ...orders]);
   249	  }
   250	  return byTick;
   251	}

  3292	        point,

  3294	        maxDistance,

  3992	  /**

  8227	  private nearestProspectorRepairTarget(): { id: BuildableId; index: number; position: ProspectorPoint } | null {

  8236	      }
  8237	    }
